The setup-offer Mac scripted job passes, including native accepted-version ownership, nonmutating setup offers, stale-choice refusal, the corrected corrupt-history retry and explicit repair with failed pending settings. Its rendering and no-Metal repeat also pass. This confirms the earlier fixture diagnosis and runtime recovery correction, within the model-free scope of those checks. Physical setup/activation and the subsequently added production setup-view rendering remain pending.

The same commit's Xcode job fails because the app target is outside the runtime package boundary and AppModel reads ModelSetup's package-scoped pack while comparing recipes. The SwiftPM app build did not expose that boundary. The corrected API exposes only the immutable startup-recipe identity as public read-only metadata; AppModel compares that value and keeps the pack storage package-scoped. No permission, registry, selection, download or runtime policy changes. This failed Xcode receipt is preserved, and the correction still requires both native build paths to pass.
